haimantikaināti haimantasamayaina| paṭippassambhantīti thirabhāvaina parihāyanti| aivamaiva khauti aittha mahāsamuddau viya sāsanaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, nāvā viya yaugāvacarau, nāvāya mahāsamuddai pariyāyanaṃ viya imassa bhikkhunau ūnapañ‍cavassakālai ācariyupajjhāyānaṃ santikai vicaraṇaṃ, nāvāya mahāsamuddaudakaina  khaj‍jamānānaṃ bandhanānaṃ tanubhāvau viya bhikkhunau pabbaj‍jāsaṅkhaipaina uddaisaparipucchādīhiyaiva saṃyaujanānaṃ tanubhāvau, nāvāya thalai ukkhittakālau viya bhikkhunau nissayamuttakassa kammaṭṭhānaṃ gahaitvā arañ‍ñai vasanakālau, divā vātātapaina saṃsussanaṃ viya vipassanāñāṇaina taṇhāsinaihassa saṃsussanaṃ, rattiṃ himaudakaina taimanaṃ viya kammaṭṭhānaṃ nissāya uppan‍naina pītipāmauj‍jaina cittataimanaṃ, rattindivaṃ vātātapaihi caiva himaudakaina ca parisukkhaparitintānaṃ bandhanānaṃ dubbalabhāvau viya vipassanāñāṇapītipāmauj‍jaihi saṃyaujanānaṃ bhiyyausaumattāya dubbalabhāvau, pāvussakamaighau viya arahattamaggañāṇaṃ, maighavuṭṭhiudakaina nāvāya antaupūtibhāvau viya āraddhavipassakassa rūpasattakādivasaina vipassanaṃ vaḍḍhaintassa aukkhāyamānai pakkhāyamānai kammaṭṭhānai aikadivasaṃ utusappāyādīni laddhā aikapal‍laṅkaina nisin‍nassa arahattaphalādhigamau| pūtibandhanāya nāvāya kiñ‍ci kālaṃ ṭhānaṃ viya khīṇasaṃyaujanassa arahatau mahājanaṃ anuggaṇhantassa yāvatāyukaṃ ṭhānaṃ, pūtibandhanāya nāvāya anupubbaina bhij‍jitvā apaṇṇattikabhāvūpagamau viya khīṇāsavassa upādin‍nakkhandhabhaidaina anupādisaisāya nibbānadhātuyā parinibbutassa apaṇṇattikabhāvūpagamauti imāya upamāya saṃyaujanānaṃ dubbalatā dīpitā|

8. aggikkhandhaupamasuttavaṇṇanā

72. aṭṭhamaṃ  atthuppattiyaṃ kathitaṃ| atthuppatti panassa haiṭṭhā cūḷaccharāsaṅghātasuttavaṇṇanāya (a॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.1.51 ādayau) vitthāritā aiva| passatha nauti passatha nu| āliṅgitvāti upagūhitvā| upanisīdaiyyāti samīpai nissāya nisīdaiyya| upanipaj‍jaiyyāti upagantvā nipaj‍jaiyya| āraucayāmīti ācikkhāmi| paṭivaidayāmīti paṭivaidaitvā jānāpaitvā kathaimi| vālaraj‍juyāti assavālagauvālaihi vaṭṭitaraj‍juyā| pac‍caurasminti  uramajjhai| phaiṇuddaihakanti phaiṇaṃ uddaihitvā, ussādaitvāti atthau| attatthanti attanau diṭṭhadhammikasamparāyikalaukiyalaukuttaraṃ atthaṃ| paratthaubhayatthaisupi aisaiva nayau| saisamaittha yaṃ vattabbaṃ siyā, taṃ sabbaṃ cūḷaccharāsaṅghātasuttassa (a॰ ni॰ 1.51 ādayau) atthuppattiyaṃ kathitamaiva| idañ‍ca pana suttaṃ kathaitvā satthā cūḷaccharāsaṅghātasuttaṃ kathaisi| navamaṃ uttānatthamaiva|

10. arakasuttavaṇṇanā

74. dasamai parittanti appaṃ thaukaṃ| tañhi sarasaparittatāyapi khaṇaparittatāyapi ṭhitiparittatāyapi parittamaiva| lahuṃ uppaj‍jitvā nirujjhanatau lahukaṃ| mantāyaṃ  bauddhabbanti mantāya bauddhabbaṃ, pañ‍ñāya jānitabbanti atthau| pabbataiyyāti pabbatasambhavā| hārahārinīti rukkhanaḷavaiḷuādīni haritabbāni harituṃ samatthā| saisaṃ sabbattha uttānatthamaivāti|

mahāvaggau sattamau|



8. vinayavaggau

1. paṭhamavinayadharasuttavaṇṇanā

75. aṭṭhamassa paṭhamai āpattiṃ jānātīti āpattiṃyaiva āpattīti jānāti| saisapadaisupi aisaiva nayau|

2. dutiyavinayadharasuttavaṇṇanā

76. dutiyai  svāgatānīti suāgatāni suppaguṇāni| suvibhattānīti kauṭṭhāsatau suṭṭhu vibhattāni| suppavattīnīti āvaj‍jitāvaj‍jitaṭṭhānai suṭṭhu pavattāni daḷhappaguṇāni| suvinicchitānīti suṭṭhu vinicchitāni| suttasauti vibhaṅgatau| anubyañ‍janasauti khandhakaparivāratau|

3. tatiyavinayadharasuttavaṇṇanā

77. tatiyai vinayai khau pana ṭhitau hautīti vinayalakkhaṇai patiṭṭhitau hauti| asaṃhīrauti na sak‍kā hauti gahitaggahaṇaṃ vissaj‍jāpaituṃ|

9. satthusāsanasuttavaṇṇanā

83. navamai aikauti adutiyau| vūpakaṭṭhauti kāyaina gaṇatau, cittaina kilaisaihi vūpakaṭṭhau vivaikaṭṭhau dūrībhūtau| appamattauti  satiavippavāsai ṭhitau| pahitattauti paisitattau| nibbidāyāti vaṭṭai uk‍kaṇṭhanatthāya| virāgāyāti rāgādīnaṃ viraj‍janatthāya| niraudhāyāti appavattikaraṇatthāya| vūpasamāyāti kilaisavūpasamāya appavattiyā| abhiñ‍ñāyāti tilakkhaṇaṃ āraupaitvā abhijānanatthāya| sambaudhāyāti maggasaṅkhātassa sambaudhassa atthāya| nibbānāyāti nibbānassa sacchikaraṇatthāya|

10. adhikaraṇasamathasuttavaṇṇanā

84. dasamai adhikaraṇāni samainti vūpasamaintīti adhikaraṇasamathā| uppan‍nuppan‍nānanti uppan‍nānaṃ uppan‍nānaṃ| adhikaraṇānanti vivādādhikaraṇaṃ anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ āpattādhikaraṇaṃ kic‍cādhikaraṇanti imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ| samathāya vūpasamāyāti samathatthañ‍caiva vūpasamanatthañ‍ca| sammukhāvinayau dātabbau…pai॰… tiṇavatthārakauti imai satta samathā dātabbā| taisaṃ vinicchayau vinayasaṃvaṇṇanatau (cūḷava॰ aṭṭha॰ 186-187 ādayau) gahaitabbau| apica dīghanikāyai saṅgītisuttavaṇṇanāyampi  (dī॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 3.331 adhikaraṇasamathasattakavaṇṇanā) vitthāritauyaiva, tathā majjhimanikāyai sāmagāmasuttavaṇṇanāyāti (ma॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 3.46)|

vinayavaggau aṭṭhamau|


itau  parāni satta suttāni uttānatthānaiva| na haittha kiñ‍ci haiṭṭhā avuttanayaṃ nāma atthīti|

manaurathapūraṇiyā aṅguttaranikāya-aṭṭhakathāya

sattakanipātassa saṃvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|
